         expanding the scope of businesses, mainly in organic and polymer chemistry. We are continuing to refine                                                                                                                                                       Enhanced competitive power

         Message from the President


                                                                                                                    Management Approach


                                                                                                                        Foster organizational culture change – Develop human resources with
                                                                                                                                       high aspirations and a sense of mission

                                                                                                                    It has been more than 10 years since I became Tokuyama’s             businesses, but there is always room to improve and
                                                                                                                    President and Executive Officer in March 2015. I have                become more competitive so long as we refuse to be

                                                                                                                                                 Segment).                                                                                                                                                       significant time constraints and thus tend to focus solely on

          Management Team (As of June 24, 2025)

                                                                                                                                                                                                               Reason for Skill Selection
                                                                                                                                                                                                                            Skill                                                      Definition of skill and reason for selection

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            The Company considers that having one or more directors with extensive management experience and broad insights in
                                                                                                                                                                                                               Management planning and
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            this area is indispensable when determining management plans and strategic policies, including portfolio management, in
                                                                                                                                                                                                               strategy
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            order for the Company to achieve sustainable growth.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            Based on the value of “Commitment to customer satisfaction as a profit source,” the Company considers that having one
                                                                                                                                                                                                               Sales and marketing          or more directors with extensive experience and broad insights in this area is necessary for marketing, building businesses
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            and creating profits based on an accurate understanding of the business environment.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          As a comprehensive chemical manufacturer, the Company considers that having one or more directors with a track record
                                                                                                                                                                                                               R&D, production technology
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          of promoting various innovations and extensive experience and broad insights in R&D, production technology and
                                                                                                                                                                                                               and engineering
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          engineering is necessary in order to create new business opportunities based on proprietary technologies.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            The Company considers that, both for accurate financial reporting and the formulation of a financial strategy that facilitates
                                                                                                                                                                                                               Finance and accounting       both investment for sustainable growth and shareholder returns while building a strong financial foundation, having one or
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            more directors with extensive experience and broad insights in this area is necessary.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            The Company considers that the establishment of an appropriate governance system is the foundation for all corporate
                                                                                                                                                                                                               Governance and risk          activities and that having one or more directors with extensive experience and broad insights in areas such as corporate
                                                                                                                                                                                                               management                   governance, risk management, legal affairs and compliance is necessary in order to improve the effectiveness of
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            management and supervision by the Board of Directors.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            In order to contribute to solving various social issues through its business and to be a company that is both trusted and
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            needed by society, and given that this requires expertise from a sustainability management perspective pertaining to the
                                                                                                                                                                                                               Sustainability
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            environment and society, the Company considers that having one or more directors with extensive experience and broad
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            insights in this area is necessary.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            The Company recognizes human resources as being the most critically important management capital for sustainable
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            growth, and considers that having one or more directors with extensive experience and broad insights in this area is
          (Back row, from left) Takahide Taniguchi, Shiro Saito, Tomohiro Inoue, Hiroshi Yokota, Naoki Kondo, Kazumasa Sueoka                                                                                  Human capital
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            necessary in order to build a human resources strategy that is linked to our business strategy and to be a company where
          (Front row, from left) Hiraku Ishizuka, Youji Miyamoto, Nobuko Mizumoto, Fumiaki Iwasaki, Yumiko Kajiwara
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            diverse human resources can harness their respective skills to the maximum extent.
